<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $96$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out &#36;\dfrac{3}{4}&#36; of &#36;96&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 96 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 96 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 96} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 24 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 24 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 24 \\\\
&= 72
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$72$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

</code></pre>

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

</span>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write down the next number in the sequence.

$83, \quad 76, \quad 69, \quad 62, \quad \boxed{\phantom{11}}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write down the next number in the sequence.

&#36;83, \quad 76, \quad 69, \quad 62, \quad \boxed{\phantom{11}}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: Look at how the terms change.


Subtract $7$ each time.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Pattern</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \sequenceStep{-7}
&&  \sequenceStep{-7}
&&  \sequenceStep{-7}
&&  \sequenceStep{-7}  \\
83,  &&   76,  &&   69,  &&   62,  &&   \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Missing number</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Missing number} &= 62 - 7 \\\\
                      &= 55
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write the missing sign $( = , <$ or $>)$ in the box.

$16 \times 7  \ \boxed{\phantom{11}} \ 17 \times 6$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write the missing sign &#36;( = , &lt;&#36; or &#36;&gt;)&#36; in the box.

&#36;16 \times 7  \ \boxed{\phantom{11}} \ 17 \times 6&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Compare</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Left}  &= 16 \times 7 \\\\
             &= 112 \\\\
\text{Right} &= 17 \times 6 \\\\
             &= 102
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$16 \times 7 \quad \boxedFilledValue{>} \quad 17 \times 6$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In Moscow, the temperature is $-11 \degree \text{C}$ and in Paris the temperature is $14 \ \text{degrees}$ warmer.


What is the temperature in Paris?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In Moscow, the temperature is &#36;-11 \degree \text{C}&#36; and in Paris the temperature is &#36;14 \ \text{degrees}&#36; warmer.


What is the temperature in Paris?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Paris} &= \text{Moscow} + 14 \\\\
              &= -11 + 14 \\\\
              &= 3 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question-nav">

<p>the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2016--group-1:1:11</p>

<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_ng3</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>
</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.algebra
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Aisha thinks of a number.

She adds four and then multiplies by $8$.

Her result is $56$.


What was the number Aisha first thought of? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Aisha thinks of a number.

She adds four and then multiplies by &#36;8&#36;.

Her result is &#36;56&#36;.


What was the number Aisha first thought of? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
(a + 4) \times 8  &= 56 \\\\
a + 4             &= \dfrac {56} {8} \\\\
a + 4             &= \dfrac {8 \times 7} {8} \\\\
a + 4             &= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 7} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
a + 4             &= 7 \\\\
a                 &= 7 - 4 \\\\
a                 &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number $= 3$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the number &#36;= a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
(a + 4) \times 8  &= 56 \\\\
a + 4             &= \dfrac {56} {8} \\\\
a + 4             &= \dfrac {8 \times 7} {8} \\\\
a + 4             &= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 7} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
a + 4             &= 7 \\\\
a                 &= 7 - 4 \\\\
a                 &= 3
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number &#36;= 3&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</span>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Georgie left home at $7{:}55 \ \text{am}$ and reached school $45 \ \text{minutes}$ later.

At what time did Georgie reach school?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Georgie left home at &#36;7{:}55 \ \text{am}&#36; and reached school &#36;45 \ \text{minutes}&#36; later.

At what time did Georgie reach school?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival time} &= 7{:}55 \ \text{am} + 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 8{:}40 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8{:}40 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.number.fraction
</span>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the difference between a tenth of $5$ and a fifth of $10$? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the difference between a tenth of &#36;5&#36; and a fifth of &#36;10&#36;? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &=  \dfrac {1} {5} \ \text {of} \ 10 - \dfrac {1} {10} \ \text {of} \ 5 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {5} \times 10 - \dfrac {1} {10} \times 5 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {10} {5} - \dfrac {5} {10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 2} {5} - \dfrac {5} {5 \times 2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 2} {\cancel{5}} - \dfrac {\cancel{5}} {\cancel{5} \times 2} \\\\
                    &= 2 - \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \times 2} {2} - \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2} {2} - \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2 - 1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
                    &= 1 \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1 \dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &=  \dfrac {1} {5} \ \text {of} \ 10 - \dfrac {1} {10} \ \text {of} \ 5 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {5} \times 10 - \dfrac {1} {10} \times 5 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {10} {5} - \dfrac {5} {10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 2} {5} - \dfrac {5} {5 \times 2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 2} {\cancel{5}} - \dfrac {\cancel{5}} {\cancel{5} \times 2} \\\\
                    &= 2 - \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \times 2} {2} - \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2} {2} - \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2 - 1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
                    &= 1 \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;1 \dfrac {1} {2}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
(a + 7) \times 4 &= 120 \\\\
a + 7            &= \dfrac {120} {4} \\\\
a + 7            &= \dfrac {30 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a + 7            &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a + 7            &= 30 \\\\
a                &= 30 - 7 \\\\
a                &= 23
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Input $= 23$

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There are two possible function machines that will give the results shown below.

Work out the rules for the two possible machines.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

There are two possible cases.

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 1</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The machine could add first and then multiply.

Let the first operation be $+a$

Let the second operation be $\times b$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 1 equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
(5 + a) \times b &= 4  & (1) \\\\
(8 + a) \times b &= 10 & (2)
\end{aligned}
$

Subtract equation $(1)$ from equation $(2)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
(8 + a)b - (5 + a)b &= 10 - 4 \\\\
8b + ab - 5b - ab   &= 6 \\\\
3b                   &= 6 \\\\
b                    &= \dfrac {6} {3} \\\\
b                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 3} {3} \\\\
b                    &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
b                    &= 2
\end{aligned}
$

Substitute into equation $(1)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
(5 + a) \times 2 &= 4 \\\\
5 + a            &= \dfrac {4} {2} \\\\
5 + a            &= 2 \\\\
a                &= 2 - 5 \\\\
a                &= -3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 1 answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

One possible machine is $+(-3)$ and then $\times 2$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 2</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The machine could multiply first and then add.

Let the first operation be $\times c$

Let the second operation be $+d$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 2 equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
5c + d &= 4  & (1) \\\\
8c + d &= 10 & (2)
\end{aligned}
$

Subtract equation $(1)$ from equation $(2)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
8c + d - (5c + d) &= 10 - 4 \\\\
8c + d - 5c - d   &= 6 \\\\
3c                 &= 6 \\\\
c                  &= \dfrac {6} {3} \\\\
c                  &= 2
\end{aligned}
$

Substitute into equation $(1)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
5 \times 2 + d &= 4 \\\\
10 + d         &= 4 \\\\
d              &= 4 - 10 \\\\
d              &= -6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 2 answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Another possible machine is $\times 2$ and then $+(-6)$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$+(-3) \ \text{then} \ \times 2$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\times 2 \ \text{then} \ +(-6)$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jasmine has the six number cards shown below.

$\Huge{\boxed{1} \ \boxed{9} \ \boxed{5} \ \boxed{3} \ \boxed{8} \ \boxed{4}}$

The cards can be placed side by side to form different numbers.

For example, using four of the cards, the largest $4$-digit even number that can be made is $9854$.

$\Huge{\boxed{9} \ \boxed{8} \ \boxed{5} \ \boxed{4}}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the largest $3$-digit multiple of $3$ that can be made?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the largest &#36;3&#36;-digit multiple of &#36;3&#36; that can be made?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

- ABBR: As we know that a number is divisible by 3, if the sum of all its digits is divisible by 3.

- ABBR: To make the largest number, put the largest digit in the highest place value, then the next largest digit, and so on.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Choose the first digits</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

To make the largest $3$-digit number, start with the largest cards.

The two largest cards are $9$ and $8$, and $9 + 8 = 17$.

The next largest card that makes the digit sum a multiple of $3$ is $4$, because $9 + 8 + 4 = 21$.
</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Largest number</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

So the largest possible $3$-digit multiple of $3$ is $984$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$984$

</div>
</div>

To make the largest &#36;3&#36;-digit number, start with the largest cards.

The two largest cards are &#36;9&#36; and &#36;8&#36;, and &#36;9 + 8 = 17&#36;.

The next largest card that makes the digit sum a multiple of &#36;3&#36; is &#36;4&#36;, because &#36;9 + 8 + 4 = 21&#36;.
&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Largest number"&gt;

So the largest possible &#36;3&#36;-digit multiple of &#36;3&#36; is &#36;984&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a magic square the sum of the numbers in each row, column and diagonal is the same.

The numerals $1$ to $9$ are used in this magic square.

Complete the magic square.

|   |$5$|   |

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$38 \times 42$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;38 \times 42&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 38 \times 42 \\\\
&= (37 + 1) \times 42 \\\\
&= 37 \times 42 + 1 \times 42 \\\\
&= 1554 + 42 \\\\
&= 1596
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$37 \times 21$

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 37 \times 21 \\\\
&= 37 \times \dfrac {42} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times 42} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1554} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {777 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {777 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 777
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 155.4 \div 42 \\\\
&= \dfrac {155.4} {42} \\\\
&= \dfrac {155.4 \times 10} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1554} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times 42} {42 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37 \times \cancel{42}} {\cancel{42} \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {37} {10} \\\\
&= 3.7
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the ingredients needed to make this sorbet for $15$ people.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the ingredients needed to make this sorbet for &#36;15&#36; people.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{people}     &:& \text{water}                                         &:& \text{sugar}                                                                          &:& \text{strawberries}                                  &:& \text{eggs} \\\\
= & 6                 &:& 300 \ \text{ml}                                      &:& 100 \ \text{g}                                                                        &:& 450 \ \text{g}                                       &:& 2 \\\\
= & 1                 &:& \dfrac {300} {6}                                     &:& \dfrac {100} {6}                                                                      &:& \dfrac {450} {6}                                     &:& \dfrac {2} {6} \\\\
= & 1 \times 15       &:& \dfrac {300 \times 15} {6}                           &:& \dfrac {100 \times 15} {6}                                                            &:& \dfrac {450 \times 15} {6}                           &:& \dfrac {2 \times 15} {6} \\\\
= & 15                &:& \dfrac {6 \times 50 \times 15} {6}                   &:& \dfrac {50 \times 2 \times 5 \times 3} {3 \times 2}                                   &:& \dfrac {6 \times 75 \times 15} {6}                   &:& \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 5} {3 \times 2} \\\\
= & 15                &:& \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 50 \times 15} {\cancel 6}   &:& \dfrac {50 \times \cancel 2 \times 5 \times \cancel 3} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2}   &:& \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 75 \times 15} {\cancel 6}   &:& \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 3 \times 5} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2} \\\\
= & 15                &:& 50 \times 15                                         &:& 50 \times 5                                                                           &:& 75 \times 15                                         &:& 5 \\\\
= & 15                &:& 750 \ \text{ml}                                      &:& 250 \ \text{g}                                                                        &:& 1125 \ \text{g}                                      &:& 5
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Water $=$ $750 \ \text{ml}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Sugar $=$ $250 \ \text{g}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Strawberries $=$ $1125 \ \text{g}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Eggs $=$ $5$

</div>
</div>

= & 15                &:& \dfrac {6 \times 50 \times 15} {6}                   &:& \dfrac {50 \times 2 \times 5 \times 3} {3 \times 2}                                   &:& \dfrac {6 \times 75 \times 15} {6}                   &:& \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 5} {3 \times 2} \\\\
= & 15                &:& \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 50 \times 15} {\cancel 6}   &:& \dfrac {50 \times \cancel 2 \times 5 \times \cancel 3} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2}   &:& \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 75 \times 15} {\cancel 6}   &:& \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 3 \times 5} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2} \\\\

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion" id="question-the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium-11-plus-maths-2016-group-1-1-19-2">
<div class="uuid">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
family.recipe
</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.ratio
</span>

</div>
<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

For how many people is Lily making this sorbet if she uses exactly $1 \ \text{kilogram}$ of sugar?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">For how many people is Lily making this sorbet if she uses exactly &#36;1 \ \text{kilogram}&#36; of sugar?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{sugar}                               &:& \text{people} \\\\
= & 100 \ \text{g}                             &:& 6 \\\\
= & 100 \times 10                              &:& 6 \times 10 \\\\
= & 1000 \ \text{g}                            &:& 60 \\\\
= & 1 \ \text{kg}                              &:& 60
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of people $= 60$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of people Lily served sorbet $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solve the ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
100 \ \text{g} : 6 \ \text{people}              &= 1 \ \text{kg} : a \ \text{people} \\\\
\dfrac{100 \ \text{g}} {6}                      &= \dfrac {1 \ \text{kg}} {a} \\\\
\dfrac{100 \ \text{g}} {6}                      &= \dfrac {1000 \ \text{g}} {a} \\\\
100 \times a                                    &= 1000 \times 6 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {1000 \times 6} {100} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0 \times 6} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
a                                               &= 10 \times 6 \\\\
a                                               &= 60
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of people $= 60$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the number of people Lily served sorbet &#36;= a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Solve the ratio"&gt;

Using equivalent ratio,

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
100 \ \text{g} : 6 \ \text{people}              &= 1 \ \text{kg} : a \ \text{people} \\\\
\dfrac{100 \ \text{g}} {6}                      &= \dfrac {1 \ \text{kg}} {a} \\\\
\dfrac{100 \ \text{g}} {6}                      &= \dfrac {1000 \ \text{g}} {a} \\\\
100 \times a                                    &= 1000 \times 6 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {1000 \times 6} {100} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {10 \cancel 0 \cancel 0 \times 6} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
a                                               &= 10 \times 6 \\\\
a                                               &= 60
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

<div class="question_envelope rag_ng3 rag_prns question" id="question-the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium-11-plus-maths-2016-group-1-1-20">

<div class="papername">
<p>the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium--11-plus--maths--2016--group-1:1:20</p>

</div>
<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.algebra
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Celia and Autumn have each thought of an integer (whole number) between $0$ and $20$.

The product of their numbers is $72$ and the difference between the numbers is the same as Celia's number.

What is Autumn's number? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Celia and Autumn have each thought of an integer (whole number) between &#36;0&#36; and &#36;20&#36;.

The product of their numbers is &#36;72&#36; and the difference between the numbers is the same as Celia's number.

What is Autumn's number? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let Celia's number $= a$

As the difference between the numbers is the same as Celia's number, Autumn's number $= a + a = 2a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 2a  &= 72 \\\\
2a^2         &= 72 \\\\
a^2          &= \dfrac {72} {2} \\\\
a^2          &= \dfrac {36 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a^2          &= \dfrac {36 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a^2          &= 36 \\\\
a            &= \sqrt {36} \\\\
a            &= 6 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Celia's number}  &= 6 \\\\
\text{Autumn's number} &= 2a \\\\
                       &= 2 \times 6 \\\\
                       &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kiwi fruits cost $78 \ \text{p}$ each; cherries cost $8 \ \text{p}$ each.

Gina buys as many kiwi fruits as she can for $\pounds 5$, and spends all of her change on cherries. 

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many kiwi fruits does Gina buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many kiwi fruits does Gina buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Kiwis possible} &= \dfrac {\pounds 5} {78 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {500 \ \text{p}} {78 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                      &= 6 \ \text{remainder} \ 32 \\\\
\text{Kiwis bought}   &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

                      &= \dfrac {500 \ \text{p}} {78 \ \text{p}} \\\\

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many cherries does Gina buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many cherries does Gina buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money left}         &= 500  - 6 \times 78  \\\\
                          &= 500  - 468  \\\\
                          &= 32 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Cherries Gina buys} &= \dfrac {32 } {8} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {8} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 4} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
                          &= 4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

</span>
</li>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

James missed the $4.20 \ \text{p.m.}$ train by three minutes.


|             |       Train A      |       Train B      |
|-----------|------------------|------------------|
|    London   | $4.20 \ \text{p.m.}$ | $5.02 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
| Staplehurst | $5.16 \ \text{p.m.}$ | $5.59 \ \text{p.m.}$ |


How long must he wait to catch the $5.02 \ \text{p.m.}$ train?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">James missed the &#36;4.20 \ \text{p.m.}&#36; train by three minutes.

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

|             |       Train A      |       Train B      |
|-----------|------------------|------------------|
|    London   | &#36;4.20 \ \text{p.m.}&#36; | &#36;5.02 \ \text{p.m.}&#36; |
| Staplehurst | &#36;5.16 \ \text{p.m.}&#36; | &#36;5.59 \ \text{p.m.}&#36; |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

How long must he wait to catch the &#36;5.02 \ \text{p.m.}&#36; train?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{James arrives} &= 4{:}20 \ \text{pm} + 3 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                     &= 4{:}23 \ \text{pm} \\\\
\text{Waiting time}  &= 5{:}02 \ \text{pm} - 4{:}23 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                     &= 39 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$39 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A square has perimeter $20 \ \text{cm}$.

Work out the area of the square.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A square has perimeter &#36;20 \ \text{cm}&#36;.

Work out the area of the square.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the side length of the square $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Side length</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times a                  &= 20 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                           &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area} &= a \times a \\\\
            &= 5 \times 5 \\\\
            &= 25 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$25 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_square_expanded
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_expanded_perimeter_square
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the side length of the square &#36;= a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Side length"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
4 \times a                  &= 20 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {20} {4} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                           &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area} &= a \times a \\\\
            &= 5 \times 5 \\\\
            &= 25 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Joanne's marks in five spelling tests are: 

$7 \quad 9 \quad 8 \quad 7 \quad 6$

What is the range of her marks?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Joanne's marks in five spelling tests are: 

&#36;7 \quad 9 \quad 8 \quad 7 \quad 6&#36;

What is the range of her marks?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

- ABBR: $\text{Range} = \text{Highest number} - \text{Lowest number}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Highest value} &= 9 \\\\
\text{Lowest value}  &= 6 \\\\
\text{Range}         &= 9 - 6 \\\\
                     &= 3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">
- rtq_abbr_formula_md_statistics_formula_range

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Highest value} &= 9 \\\\
\text{Lowest value}  &= 6 \\\\
\text{Range}         &= 9 - 6 \\\\
                     &= 3
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;3&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Charlotte removes a third of the woodlice from pot $B$ and $20\%$ of the woodlice from pot $C$. 
She puts these woodlice under a new pot, $F$.

How many woodlice are under pot $F$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Charlotte removes a third of the woodlice from pot &#36;B&#36; and &#36;20\%&#36; of the woodlice from pot &#36;C&#36;. 
She puts these woodlice under a new pot, &#36;F&#36;.

How many woodlice are under pot &#36;F&#36;?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Pot counts</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Pot B}  &= 4.5 \times 2 \\\\
              &= 9 \\\\
\text{Pot C}  &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
              &= 10
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Moved woodlice</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Moved from B} &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 9 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {9} {3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                    &= 3 \\\\
\text{Moved from C} &= 20 \% \times 10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {20 \times 10} {100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 1 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 2 \\\\
\text{Pot F}        &= 3 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Pot counts"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Pot B}  &= 4.5 \times 2 \\\\
              &= 9 \\\\
\text{Pot C}  &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
              &= 10
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Moved woodlice"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Moved from B} &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 9 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {9} {3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                    &= 3 \\\\
\text{Moved from C} &= 20 \% \times 10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 10 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {20 \times 10} {100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 1 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 2 \\\\
\text{Pot F}        &= 3 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 5
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;5&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mum, Dad and Granny have three cakes:

Chocolate, strawberry and coffee.

Mum likes chocolate or coffee.

Dad likes chocolate, and Granny likes all three.

How can it be arranged, so that they each get a cake that they like? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mum, Dad and Granny have three cakes:

Chocolate, strawberry and coffee.

Mum likes chocolate or coffee.

Dad likes chocolate, and Granny likes all three.

How can it be arranged, so that they each get a cake that they like? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Dad must have the chocolate cake, because that is the only cake he likes.

Mum can then have the coffee cake.

Granny can have the strawberry cake, because Granny likes all three cakes.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Dad $=$ Chocolate

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Mum $=$ Coffee

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Granny $=$ Strawberry

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

mark with letter $A$ the likelihood that the spinner lands on an even number.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">mark with letter &#36;A&#36; the likelihood that the spinner lands on an even number.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Even numbers</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Even numbers} &= 10 \\\\
\text{P(even number)} &= \dfrac {10} {20} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {10} {2 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0}} {2 \times 1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Scale</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Mark $A$ at even chance.

</div>

$A$ at even chance

</div>
</div>

                    &= \dfrac {10} {2 \times 10} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0}} {2 \times 1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {2}

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

mark with letter $B$ the likelihood that the spinner lands on a multiple of $5$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

</code></pre>

</div>
