<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Multiples of 5} &= 5, 10, 15, 20 \\\\
\text{Count}          &= 4 \\\\
\text{P(multiple of 5)} &= \dfrac {4} {20} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {4} {5 \times 4} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {\cancel {4}} {5 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {1} {5}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Zoya has a jug of capacity $1.5 \ \text{litres}$ which is full of lemonade.

She has eight cups each of capacity $150 \ \text{ml}$.

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What volume of lemonade is left in the jug?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What volume of lemonade is left in the jug?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $1 \ \text{l} = 1000 \ \text{ml}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Jug capacity}    &= 1.5 \times 1000 \ \text{ml} \\\\
                       &= 1500 \ \text{ml} \\\\
\text{Lemonade poured} &= 8 \times 150 \ \text{ml} \\\\
                       &= 1200 \ \text{ml} \\\\
\text{Lemonade left}   &= 1500 - 1200 \\\\
                       &= 300 \ \text{ml}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$300 \ \text{ml}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Zoya has five $2 \ \text{litre}$ bottles of lemonade

How many times could Zoya completely fill her jug from these bottles?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Zoya has five &#36;2 \ \text{litre}&#36; bottles of lemonade

How many times could Zoya completely fill her jug from these bottles?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

A partial jug is not completely filled, so only the whole number counts.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Calculation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total lemonade} &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
                      &= 10 \ \text{litres} \\\\
\text{Jugs filled}   &= \dfrac {10} {1.5} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {10 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {10 \times 5 \times 2} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {10 \times \cancel {5} \times 2} {3 \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {20} {3} \\\\
                      &= 6 \dfrac {2} {3} \\\\
\text{Complete fills} &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Calculation"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total lemonade} &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
                      &= 10 \ \text{litres} \\\\
\text{Jugs filled}   &= \dfrac {10} {1.5} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {10 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {10 \times 5 \times 2} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {10 \times \cancel {5} \times 2} {3 \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {20} {3} \\\\
                      &= 6 \dfrac {2} {3} \\\\
\text{Complete fills} &= 6
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the perimeter of the card?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the perimeter of the card?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Perimeter} &= 2 \times (18 + 6) \\\\
                 &= 2 \times 24 \\\\
                 &= 48 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rectangular stickers measure $3 \ \text{cm}$ by $2 \ \text{cm}$.

Susan wants to cover the front of the card with stickers, without any overlapping.

What is the maximum number of stickers she can fit on the front of the card?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rectangular stickers measure &#36;3 \ \text{cm}&#36; by &#36;2 \ \text{cm}&#36;.

Susan wants to cover the front of the card with stickers, without any overlapping.

What is the maximum number of stickers she can fit on the front of the card?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Stickers} &= \dfrac {\text{Area of card}} {\text{Area of a sticker}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {18 \times 6} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {6 \times 3 \times 3 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {3} \times 3 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                &= 18
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$18$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Stickers} &= \dfrac {\text{Area of card}} {\text{Area of a sticker}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {18 \times 6} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {6 \times 3 \times 3 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {3} \times 3 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                &= 18
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Cube $A$ has a volume of $8 \ \text{cm}^3$.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2016/group-1/questions/s01-q38-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div> 


Another cube, $B$, has edges which are twice the length of the edges of cube $A$.

Work out the volume of cube $B$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Cube &#36;A&#36; has a volume of &#36;8 \ \text{cm}^3&#36;.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt; 


Another cube, &#36;B&#36;, has edges which are twice the length of the edges of cube &#36;A&#36;.

Work out the volume of cube &#36;B&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the edge length of cube $A = a$

Edge length of cube $B = 2a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a^3                &= 8 \\\\
\text{Cube B volume}  &= (2a)^3 \\\\
                   &= 8a^3 \\\\
                   &= 8 \times 8 \\\\
                   &= 64 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Cube $B$ volume $= 64 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$64 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

Edge length of cube &#36;B = 2a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a^3                &= 8 \\\\
\text{Cube B volume}  &= (2a)^3 \\\\
                   &= 8a^3 \\\\
                   &= 8 \times 8 \\\\
                   &= 64 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many tea leaves would you expect there to be in a $1 \ \text{kg}$ pack of tea?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many tea leaves would you expect there to be in a &#36;1 \ \text{kg}&#36; pack of tea?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tea leaves} &= \dfrac {1000} {0.008} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {1000 \times 1000} {8} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {1000 \times 125 \times 8} {8} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {1000 \times 125 \times \cancel {8}} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
                  &= 1000 \times 125 \\\\
                  &= 125000
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$125000$

</div>

<div class="uuid">
<p>EF895ED5-3FAD-4D19-AE78-5FAAC28EBD67</p>
</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">

<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A tea bag contains $250$ tea leaves.

The tissue covering weighs $0.5 \ \text{g}$.

How many tea bags will there be in a $250 \text{g}$ packet of tea bags?

(The $250 \text{g}$ includes the mass of the bags and not just the tea leaves.)

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A tea bag contains &#36;250&#36; tea leaves.

The tissue covering weighs &#36;0.5 \ \text{g}&#36;.

How many tea bags will there be in a &#36;250 \text{g}&#36; packet of tea bags?

(The &#36;250 \text{g}&#36; includes the mass of the bags and not just the tea leaves.)
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mass of 1 bag} &= 250 \times 0.008 + 0.5 \\\\
                     &= 2 + 0.5 \\\\
                     &= 2.5 \ \text{g} \\\\
\text{Tea bags}      &= \dfrac {\text{Total mass}} {\text{Mass of 1 bag}} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {250} {2.5} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {250 \times 10} {25} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {25 \times 10 \times 10} {25} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 10 \times 10} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
                     &= 100
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100 \ \text{bags}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mass of 1 bag} &= 250 \times 0.008 + 0.5 \\\\
                     &= 2 + 0.5 \\\\
                     &= 2.5 \ \text{g} \\\\
\text{Tea bags}      &= \dfrac {\text{Total mass}} {\text{Mass of 1 bag}} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {250} {2.5} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {250 \times 10} {25} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {25 \times 10 \times 10} {25} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 10 \times 10} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
                     &= 100
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

**Question**

- Start with the pattern number, $\text{n}$.
- Add $1$ to give a new number, $\text{m}$.
- Multiply $\text{m}$ by $\text{n}$.
- Divide by $2$

Use the instructions to calculate the number of squares in pattern $100$.

</div>

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Pattern 100</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of squares} &= \dfrac{(100 + 1) \times 100}{2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{101 \times 100}{2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{101 \times 50 \times 2}{2} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{101 \times 50 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                         &= 101 \times 50 \\\\
                         &= 5050
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Three hedgehogs, Rana, Sarah and Tina, are collecting leaves.

Rana collects twice as many as Sarah.

Sarah collects one and a half times as many as Tina.

Between them they collect $198$ leaves.


How many leaves did each hedgehog collect?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Three hedgehogs, Rana, Sarah and Tina, are collecting leaves.

Rana collects twice as many as Sarah.

Sarah collects one and a half times as many as Tina.

Between them they collect &#36;198&#36; leaves.


How many leaves did each hedgehog collect?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of leaves Tina collects $= a$

Number of leaves Sarah collects $= 1.5a$

Number of leaves Rana collects $= 2 \times 1.5a = 3a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 1.5a + 3a &= 198 \\\\
5.5a &= 198 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{198}{5.5} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{1980}{55} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{5 \times 11 \times 36}{5 \times 11} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{11} \times 36}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{11}} \\\\
a &= 36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Leaves Tina collects}  &= 36 \\\\
\text{Leaves Sarah collects} &= 1.5a \\\\
                             &= 1.5 \times 36 \\\\
                             &= 54 \\\\
\text{Leaves Rana collects}  &= 3a \\\\
                             &= 3 \times 36 \\\\
                             &= 108
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Tina $=$ $36$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Sarah $=$ $54$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Rana $=$ $108$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the number of leaves Tina collects &#36;= a&#36;

Number of leaves Sarah collects &#36;= 1.5a&#36;

Number of leaves Rana collects &#36;= 2 \times 1.5a = 3a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a + 1.5a + 3a &= 198 \\\\
5.5a &= 198 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{198}{5.5} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{1980}{55} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{5 \times 11 \times 36}{5 \times 11} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{11} \times 36}{\cancel{5} \times \cancel{11}} \\\\
a &= 36
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Leaves Tina collects}  &= 36 \\\\
\text{Leaves Sarah collects} &= 1.5a \\\\
                             &= 1.5 \times 36 \\\\
                             &= 54 \\\\
\text{Leaves Rana collects}  &= 3a \\\\
                             &= 3 \times 36 \\\\
                             &= 108
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

List all the starting numbers between $10$ and $30$ inclusive which give the single digit result $4$ in a single step.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="paper-working-section-body">

To finish in one step, the square of the digit difference must be a single digit.

So the digit difference can only be $0$, $1$, $2$ or $3$.

To get $4$ in one step, the digit difference must be $2$, because $2^2 = 4$.

Between $10$ and $30$, the starting numbers with digit difference $2$ are $13$, $20$ and $24$.

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What do you think will be the most common single digit result if you calculate the result for every $2$-digit starting number?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What do you think will be the most common single digit result if you calculate the result for every &#36;2&#36;-digit starting number?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Reasoning</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The final result depends on the difference between the two digits, not on the full starting number.

From the previous parts, the possible single digit results are $0$, $1$, $4$ and $9$.

The result depends on the difference between the two digits.


| Digit difference | Number of starting numbers | Final result |
|----------------|--------------------------|------------|
| $0$ | $9$  | $0$ |
| $1$ | $17$ | $1$ |
| $2$ | $15$ | $4$ |
| $3$ | $13$ | $9$ |
| $4$ | $11$ | $9$ |
| $5$ | $9$  | $9$ |
| $6$ | $7$  | $9$ |
| $7$ | $5$  | $9$ |
| $8$ | $3$  | $4$ |
| $9$ | $1$  | $9$ |

The digit differences group like this:


| Final result | Digit differences which lead to it |
|------------|----------------------------------|
| $0$ | $0$ |
| $1$ | $1$ |
| $4$ | $2, 8$ |
| $9$ | $3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 9$ |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Count the most common result</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

For result $9$, the digit differences are $3$, $4$, $5$, $6$, $7$ and $9$.

For a digit difference of $d$, the possible digit pairs are $(0, d), (1, d + 1), \ldots, (9 - d, 9)$, so there are $10 - d$ digit pairs.

Each pair usually makes $2$ starting numbers, but the pair with $0$ only makes $1$ starting number because a number cannot start with $0$.

So the number of starting numbers is $2(10 - d) - 1$.

For example, for digit difference $3$, the digit pairs are $(0, 3), (1, 4), (2, 5), (3, 6), (4, 7), (5, 8)$ and $(6, 9)$.

These make $30$, then $14, 41$, $25, 52$, $36, 63$, $47, 74$, $58, 85$ and $69, 96$, so there are $1 + 2 \times 6 = 13$ starting numbers.


| Digit difference | Number of starting numbers |
|----------------|--------------------------|
| $3$ | $2(10 - 3) - 1 = 13$ |
| $4$ | $2(10 - 4) - 1 = 11$ |
| $5$ | $2(10 - 5) - 1 = 9$ |
| $6$ | $2(10 - 6) - 1 = 7$ |
| $7$ | $2(10 - 7) - 1 = 5$ |
| $9$ | $2(10 - 9) - 1 = 1$ |


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number giving result } 9 &= 13 + 11 + 9 + 7 + 5 + 1 \\\\
                               &= 46
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

So the most common single digit result is $9$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Reasoning"&gt;

The final result depends on the difference between the two digits, not on the full starting number.

| &#36;3&#36; | &#36;13&#36; | &#36;9&#36; |
| &#36;4&#36; | &#36;11&#36; | &#36;9&#36; |
| &#36;5&#36; | &#36;9&#36;  | &#36;9&#36; |
| &#36;6&#36; | &#36;7&#36;  | &#36;9&#36; |
| &#36;7&#36; | &#36;5&#36;  | &#36;9&#36; |
| &#36;8&#36; | &#36;3&#36;  | &#36;4&#36; |
| &#36;9&#36; | &#36;1&#36;  | &#36;9&#36; |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;
The digit differences group like this:

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

| Final result | Digit differences which lead to it |
|------------|----------------------------------|
| &#36;0&#36; | &#36;0&#36; |
| &#36;1&#36; | &#36;1&#36; |
| &#36;4&#36; | &#36;2, 8&#36; |
| &#36;9&#36; | &#36;3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 9&#36; |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Count the most common result"&gt;

For result &#36;9&#36;, the digit differences are &#36;3&#36;, &#36;4&#36;, &#36;5&#36;, &#36;6&#36;, &#36;7&#36; and &#36;9&#36;.

For a digit difference of &#36;d&#36;, the possible digit pairs are &#36;(0, d), (1, d + 1), \ldots, (9 - d, 9)&#36;, so there are &#36;10 - d&#36; digit pairs.

Each pair usually makes &#36;2&#36; starting numbers, but the pair with &#36;0&#36; only makes &#36;1&#36; starting number because a number cannot start with &#36;0&#36;.

So the number of starting numbers is &#36;2(10 - d) - 1&#36;.

For example, for digit difference &#36;3&#36;, the digit pairs are &#36;(0, 3), (1, 4), (2, 5), (3, 6), (4, 7), (5, 8)&#36; and &#36;(6, 9)&#36;.

These make &#36;30&#36;, then &#36;14, 41&#36;, &#36;25, 52&#36;, &#36;36, 63&#36;, &#36;47, 74&#36;, &#36;58, 85&#36; and &#36;69, 96&#36;, so there are &#36;1 + 2 \times 6 = 13&#36; starting numbers.

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

| Digit difference | Number of starting numbers |
|----------------|--------------------------|
| &#36;3&#36; | &#36;2(10 - 3) - 1 = 13&#36; |
| &#36;4&#36; | &#36;2(10 - 4) - 1 = 11&#36; |
| &#36;5&#36; | &#36;2(10 - 5) - 1 = 9&#36; |
| &#36;6&#36; | &#36;2(10 - 6) - 1 = 7&#36; |
| &#36;7&#36; | &#36;2(10 - 7) - 1 = 5&#36; |
| &#36;9&#36; | &#36;2(10 - 9) - 1 = 1&#36; |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number giving result } 9 &= 13 + 11 + 9 + 7 + 5 + 1 \\\\
                               &= 46
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

So the most common single digit result is &#36;9&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;9&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

math.ratio

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.algebra

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the same time that Tom can run $100 \ \text{metres}$, Jerry can run only $60 \ \text{metres}$.

Tom and Jerry have a competition.

When Jerry has run $100 \ \text{metres}$, Tom sets off in pursuit.

How many metres will Tom run before he catches Jerry?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the same time that Tom can run &#36;100 \ \text{metres}&#36;, Jerry can run only &#36;60 \ \text{metres}&#36;.

Tom and Jerry have a competition.

When Jerry has run &#36;100 \ \text{metres}&#36;, Tom sets off in pursuit.

How many metres will Tom run before he catches Jerry?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the distance Tom runs before he catches Jerry $= a$.

Then the distance Jerry runs after Tom starts $= a - 100$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equivalent ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
100  : 60      &= a  : (a - 100)  \\\\
\dfrac{100}{60}                    &= \dfrac{a}{a - 100} \\\\
100(a - 100)                       &= 60a \\\\
100a - 10000                       &= 60a \\\\
100a - 60a                         &= 10000 \\\\
40a                                &= 10000 \\\\
a                                  &= \dfrac{10000}{40} \\\\
a                                  &= \dfrac{40 \times 250}{40} \\\\
a                                  &= \dfrac{\cancel{40} \times 250}{\cancel{40}} \\\\
a                                  &= 250 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Tom will run $250 \ \text{m}$ before he catches Jerry.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$250 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the distance Tom runs before he catches Jerry &#36;= a&#36;.

Then the distance Jerry runs after Tom starts &#36;= a - 100&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equivalent ratio"&gt;

Using equivalent ratio,

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
100  : 60      &= a  : (a - 100)  \\\\
\dfrac{100}{60}                    &= \dfrac{a}{a - 100} \\\\
100(a - 100)                       &= 60a \\\\
100a - 10000                       &= 60a \\\\
100a - 60a                         &= 10000 \\\\
40a                                &= 10000 \\\\
a                                  &= \dfrac{10000}{40} \\\\
a                                  &= \dfrac{40 \times 250}{40} \\\\
a                                  &= \dfrac{\cancel{40} \times 250}{\cancel{40}} \\\\
a                                  &= 250 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Tom will run &#36;250 \ \text{m}&#36; before he catches Jerry.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;250 \ \text{m}&#36;
</code></pre>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What number is on the other side of the black counter?

</div>

</div>

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

To get the total $12$, the two counters must show either $7 + 5$ or $6 + 6$.

Since $6$ is already on the black counter and all the numbers are different, the grey counter cannot have $6$ on its other side.

Therefore the total $12$ must come from $7 + 5$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The number on the other side of the black counter is $5$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Irma gives Paula a third counter.

When Paula throws all three counters together she can get the following totals:

$13, 14, 16, 17, 20, 21, 23, 24$

What are the two numbers on the third counter?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Irma gives Paula a third counter.

What are the two numbers on the third counter?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Smallest total</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The smallest total with the third counter is $13$.

The smallest total from the first two counters is $5 + 7 = 12$.

So one side of the third counter must be $1$, because $12 + 1 = 13$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Largest total</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The largest total with the third counter is $24$.

The largest total from the first two counters is $6 + 10 = 16$.

So the other side of the third counter must be $8$, because $16 + 8 = 24$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Check that the two sides of the third counter give all the listed totals.


| Total | Combination |
|-----|-----------|
| $13$ | $5 + 7 + 1 = 13$ |
| $14$ | $6 + 7 + 1 = 14$ |
| $16$ | $5 + 10 + 1 = 16$ |
| $17$ | $6 + 10 + 1 = 17$ |
| $20$ | $5 + 7 + 8 = 20$ |
| $21$ | $6 + 7 + 8 = 21$ |
| $23$ | $5 + 10 + 8 = 23$ |
| $24$ | $6 + 10 + 8 = 24$ |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The two numbers on the third counter are $1$ and $8$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1$ and $8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Smallest total"&gt;

The smallest total with the third counter is &#36;13&#36;.

The smallest total from the first two counters is &#36;5 + 7 = 12&#36;.

So one side of the third counter must be &#36;1&#36;, because &#36;12 + 1 = 13&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Largest total"&gt;

The largest total with the third counter is &#36;24&#36;.

The largest total from the first two counters is &#36;6 + 10 = 16&#36;.

So the other side of the third counter must be &#36;8&#36;, because &#36;16 + 8 = 24&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="verify" title="Check"&gt;

Check that the two sides of the third counter give all the listed totals.

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

| Total | Combination |
|-----|-----------|
| &#36;13&#36; | &#36;5 + 7 + 1 = 13&#36; |
| &#36;14&#36; | &#36;6 + 7 + 1 = 14&#36; |
| &#36;16&#36; | &#36;5 + 10 + 1 = 16&#36; |
| &#36;17&#36; | &#36;6 + 10 + 1 = 17&#36; |
| &#36;20&#36; | &#36;5 + 7 + 8 = 20&#36; |
| &#36;21&#36; | &#36;6 + 7 + 8 = 21&#36; |
| &#36;23&#36; | &#36;5 + 10 + 8 = 23&#36; |
| &#36;24&#36; | &#36;6 + 10 + 8 = 24&#36; |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

The two numbers on the third counter are &#36;1&#36; and &#36;8&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
